Psychology Categories:
Psychological Insight -
Suzune was once a cold and selfish girl who didn’t care about anyone and refused to work as a team. At least, that’s how she was in the beginning. But over time, she changed, she learned the value of teamwork, that she wasn’t a genius who could take on everyone alone, and that she needed to connect with and appreciate the people around her. She reflected on her behavior and foolishness, overcoming her fear of speaking with her brother and reclaiming her true self.

   * Fake Camera Strategy (Y1V2): She understood the psychology of those involved: she knew that the boys in Class C would succumb to the pressure of a social authority figure like Ichinose, and she anticipated that the overwhelming heat would undermine their ability to reason logically.

   * Hide VIP Strategy (Y1V4): She understood Ichinose's psychology and used it to her advantage. When Ryuuen tried to force an alliance, Suzune warned Ichinose that switching sides would ruin her reputation for trustworthiness among the other classes, thus halting the deal.

   * 'Skill-Based' system Strategy (Y1V5): She deeply understands Sudou's psychological profile (his need for recognition and its reflection in her mistakes) and uses it to genuinely connect with him.

   * Sudou integration (Y1V2-Y2V8): Suzune realized that she and Sudou shared a similar nature (both trying to fight alone and ignoring the bigger picture). This empathy allowed her to genuinely connect with him and break the barrier of his stubbornness.

   * Trap Kushida Strategy (Y1V6): She recognized Kushida's need for validation and to maintain his untarnished reputation. She also understood Yukimura's hidden motivation behind the Sports Festival, taking advantage of it to voluntarily assign him as her tutor.

   * Expose Yamauchi (Y1V10): She accurately understood Sakayanagi's motivation for using Yamauchi as a disposable pawn, as well as the psychology of the rest of the class, who needed a valid moral justification to condemn someone to expulsion without feeling guilty. Furthermore, She understood Kushida's desperate need to maintain her reputation intact. She knew that Kushida couldn't openly defend Yamauchi without jeopardizing her own facade within the group.

   * ESE Planning (Y1V11): She fully understood Hirata's broken mental state, knowing that trying to force him or comfort him with common phrases would only push him further into his shell. She precisely identified when to push and when to delegate the problem.

Psychological Analysis -
She analyzed Kiyotaka and his different "faces" from the beginning of the series, studied the individuality and selfishness of Class D, and evaluated Sudou, his behavior, and how he would become a problem for the class.

   * Level the 3 idiots Strategy (Y1V1): She understood precisely what motivated each of them: Ike/Yamauchi's attraction to girls (via Kushida), Sudo's passion for basketball (using it as an analogy for studying), and Kiyotaka's hidden ambition/potential.

Psychological Warfare -
Suzune is capable of anticipating and guiding the thought processes of Class D’s members. Even when each individual is driven by selfish motives, she is able to align those interests and persuade them to follow a unified strategy aimed at victory.

   * Fake Camera Strategy (Y1V2): Suzune used uncomfortable environmental factors (the sweltering heat of the afternoon sun without air conditioning) to cloud the judgment of Ishizaki, Komiya, and Kondō, in addition to the pressure of facing the flawless and popular figure of Ichinose to break their mental resistance.

   * Hide Card Strategy (Y1V3): During her physical confrontation with Ibuki, she used calculated verbal provocations to break her emotionally, getting her to confess her crimes (the theft of underwear and the theft of the card).

   * Expose Yamauchi (Y1V10): She broke Yamauchi's morale by gradually exposing him until he lost his composure, blamed his own friends (Ike), and was completely disillusioned and cornered in the eyes of the group. She convinced 36 people that Yamauchi was the best choice to vote for in the "Class Poll," even when half of them had already planned and were certain about voting for Kiyotaka as a sacrificial pawn.
   *

Hot Reading -
She discovered that Kushida deeply hated her without even knowing her true mask or the reasons behind her resentment, even though all she had done was be kind and invite her out.

   * Kushida (Y1V1-Now): She used prior knowledge of Kushida's high school incident and the rumors she picked up in the past to predict how Kushida would use her classmates' information as a "weapon of mass destruction."

   * Fake Camera Strategy (Y1V2): She used her prior knowledge of the internal dynamics between Shinohara, the girls, and Ike to know exactly how to approach Ike through Kiyotaka, managing to enlist his camping skills for the group's survival.

   * Hide VIP Strategy (Y1V4): She used the information she gathered and the profile she had on Ryuuen to anticipate his provocations, counter his verbal attacks in their private meetings, and adapt her behavior.

   * Trap Kushida Strategy (Y1V6): She used her prior knowledge of Sae Chabashira's behavior from the Sudou Trial (Volume 2) to accurately predict that Sae would secretly cooperate with her plan to benefit the promotion of Class D.

   * Expose Yamauchi (Y1V10): She used her class's behavioral history and previous clues from her brother Manabu and Ayanokoji to anticipate Class A's moves and execute the confrontation at the opportune moment.
Cold Reading -
Just by observing Kiyotaka’s behavior, Suzune was able to accurately read his thoughts (false) and emotions. Even if he didn’t change his expression or reveal his intentions, she could interpret him correctly based on the little information she had about him.

   * Kushida (Y1V1-Now): Suzune is able to read the deep emotions through Kushida's eyes and non-verbal expression, noticing the firm determination of hatred in her gaze and distinguishing when her smile was hiding anger or when she was truly broken.

   * Level the 3 idiots Strategy (Y1V1): She managed to decipher the real disposition of the "three idiots" towards school and the way they reacted to social pressure or academic performance without them having to formally explain it to her.

   * Fake Camera Strategy (Y1V2): She analyzed Yahiko's behavior in the cave to detect his level of desperation and blind loyalty to Katsuragi's orders, using that tense body language to press on the exact point and extract information about the cave.

   * ESE Planning (Y1V11): During her chess match against Hashimoto, she analyzed his aggressive playing patterns and constant verbal distractions to deduce his true intentions and gauge his real skill level in real time.

Psychological Profiling -
She discovered that Kiyotaka does not have a defined personality, but rather is a malleable entity in the process of forming one. Additionally, she was able to intuit his mindset of seeing others as tools even before volume 3 and his future growth. For Suzune, Kiyotaka was no longer the boy who sought friends in the first month, nor the genius who hid behind lies to preserve his tranquility, nor the cold strategist who used others as tools to win. Kiyotaka was different, more enigmatic and void.

   * Level the 3 idiots Strategy (Y1V1): She analyzed the limitations of the three students. Instead of trying to teach Sudo the entire theory (knowing he was incapable of memorizing it all), she instructed him to strictly memorize the shortest answers with the highest score value.

   * False Cameras Strategy (Y1V2): She developed a profile of Sudo's behavior to know that she could not let him go free during the trial/resolution, deliberately keeping him in the council room to prevent him from making an impulsive mistake.

   * Fake Camera Strategy (Y1V2): She constructed a profile of Hirata's facade, identifying him as two-faced (similar to Kushida in structure). She noted that his inability to make difficult decisions or blame anyone made the class vulnerable.

   * Sudou integration (Y1V2-Y2V8): She mapped Sudou's psychology from the first month: she identified his emotional instability, his tendency to fall into external provocations (like those of Ryuuen) and his academic blocks, which allowed her to structure a tutoring plan adapted to his needs.

   * Trap Kushida Strategy (Y1V6): She developed an accurate psychological profile of Kushida, understanding that her unreasonable hatred would lead her to prioritize Suzune's expulsion above any collective benefit or harm to Class D. She also profiled Ryuuen's mindset regarding his lack of interest in traditional exams and his preference for sabotage games.

   * Expose Yamauchi (Y1V10): She drew an accurate profile of Yamauchi's vulnerabilities (his naiveté and desire for female approval with Sakayanagi), Kushida's fixation on maintaining her flawless facade, and Hirata's breaking point in the face of class division. Furthermore, she developed an accurate profile of Yamauchi, correctly inferring that he did not possess the intellectual capacity to orchestrate the plot on his own and that he had been manipulated by Sakayanagi through personal/romantic interest incentives.

   * ESE Planning (Y1V11): She created profiles of all her classmates (such as Sotomura for Judo/Typing, Miyake for Archery, Sudou for Basketball, and Matsushita for Mental Calculation) to place them in the optimal scenario. She also profiled Katsuragi to rule out using him as a spy due to his unwavering personality.

Personal ranking for the best Psychology categories:
Psychological Analysis > Psychological Profiling > Psychological Warfare > Hot Reading >= Cold Reading > Psychological Insight

Intelligence Categories:
Crystallized Intelligence -
   * ANHS income (Y1V1): Following in her brother’s footsteps, Suzune took the entrance exam for Advanced Nurturing High School, Japan’s most prestigious elite institution, designed to shape the future leaders of the country. This highly challenging test assessed proficiency in Japanese, mathematics, science, social studies, history, and English. According to her teacher, she answered almost all the questions correctly, finishing just behind first and second place by a narrow margin. Academic grades that not only remained the same, but improved over time.

   * Class D sensei (Y1V1 - Now):  Suzune has not only demonstrated strong academic competence, but has also proven her ability to teach effectively to a considerable number of individuals with very low academic ability. This was evident when she managed to improve the performance of Yamauchi, Sudou, and Ike (students with severe deficiencies in comprehension and discipline)

Additionally, on multiple occasions she organized and led study groups with the objective of raising the class’s overall academic average and preventing expulsions caused by midterm or final exams, demonstrating not only knowledge but also pedagogical and learning management skills.

   * Special Exams Compression (Y1V3 - now):  Suzune has the ability to understand the exams with just Sae's explanation, identify the loopholes in each test, and notice the unmentioned rules with which she can think of a strategy, taking advantage of all her available resources.

   * Zodiac Exam key (Y1V5): Suzune concluded that the students' last names, organized according to the order of the zodiac animals, were the key to determining the 'VIP' at the time.

   * Chess Feat (Y1V11): Suzune was able to learn a highly complex game from scratch and reach a respectable level, placing herself within the top 20% of players registered in the USCF, all within just 14 hours and without any prior experience in that type of game.

Fluid Intelligence -
   * Strategy comprehension (Y1V1-now): Suzune has demonstrated on numerous occasions that she is capable of understanding the strategies or plans devised by both allies and rivals as soon as she senses that something is off. Over time, she managed to identify behavioral and decision-making patterns, overcoming her initial tunnel vision. With the experience she accumulated, she became able not only to counter those strategies, but also to anticipate and predict them with increasing accuracy.

   * 'Skill-Based' system Strategy (Y1V5): She designed a novel quantitative system to address the Sports Festival rules as soon as Professor Sae presented the manual. She reformulated an exam traditionally based on personal effort into a problem of optimal allocation of human resources.

   * ESE Planning (Y1V11): She demonstrated an exceptional ability to solve a completely new and unforeseen problem (the game of chess at a competitive level) by applying pure logic and abstract patterns in real time against an experienced rival.
Bodily Kinesthetic Intelligence -
   * False Horikita Manabu (Y1V1): Suzune was able to keep pace with a genius like Manabu (5th dan in karate and 4th dan in aikido), taking the same martial arts classes, excelling as the top performer in athletic competitions, and demonstrating exceptional performance in virtually any sport she participated in.

   * Athletic Bodies (Y1V1): Suzune is able to identify and analyze another person's body capabilities and what they practice simply by observing the state of their muscles and body.

   * Body language (Y1V1-now): Suzune has knowledge of body language and is able to identify the various signals that humans unconsciously make, as well as control her own body.

   * Hide Card Strategy (Y1V3): Despite having a fever of 38°C, she was able to maneuver nimbly and move stealthily through the dark forest for 100 meters to track Ibuki, and subsequently engage in hand-to-hand combat with her (who is an expert in martial arts).

Linguistic Intelligence -
   * Confrontation of interviews (Y1V1): she performed just as well in the interview. She was polite, displayed excellent self-management, and proved that, based solely on her abilities, she had the potential to be placed in Class A.

   * Communication Skills (Y1V1-now): Suzune possesses the ability to express her thoughts in a clear and concise manner, adapting her speech to the circumstances, the context, and the people around her to ensure she is understood. This skill extends beyond one-on-one communication, as she is also capable of conveying her ideas to an entire class of 40 students, most of whom have a low level of comprehension, reinforcing her effectiveness as a leader and communicator.

   * Half-truths (Y1V1-now): Suzune is capable of identifying when she is being lied to or when someone is relying on half-truths. Likewise, she has mastered the strategic use of language, choosing her words with precision to guide the other person’s interpretation and make them believe exactly what she wants them to understand.

Naturalistic Intelligence -
   * Island (Y1V3): Suzune is not only capable of adapting to any situation or environment without complaint, but she can do so even under extreme physical conditions, such as having a 38 °C fever. A clear example of this occurs when Ibuki steals the card and Suzune decides to pursue her across the island despite being in a severely weakened state.

The chase took place under particularly hostile conditions: it was nighttime, heavy rain was falling, the terrain consisted of a dense forest, there was no artificial light, and Suzune was suffering from significant physical debilitation due to her fever. Even so, she demonstrated remarkable sensory and cognitive adaptability.

During the pursuit, Suzune was able to navigate forest paths without hesitation, move stealthily for approximately one hundred meters without being detected, and adjust her movement by weaving left and right, using terrain and vegetation to conceal her presence. Due to the lack of visibility, she relied primarily on sound, motion perception, and spatial memory rather than direct sight. Even while close to physical collapse, she maintained awareness of nearby individuals and was later able to identify Ryuuen’s presence.

Suzune demonstrates an advanced combination of skills, including effective navigation and stealth in a hostile natural environment, real-time interpretation of terrain, weather, and human behavior, sensory adaptation under extreme physical and environmental stress, and the integration of environmental awareness into logical and strategic reasoning.

Existential Intelligence -
   * Human value (Y1V1/Y1V5): When Kiyotaka saves her from her own brother and tries to persuade her to care about saving Sudou, Ike, and Yamauchi, Suzune begins to reflect on the concept of human value. She realizes that people cannot be judged solely by their immediate usefulness or results, but by the combination of strengths and weaknesses that define them. That duality, Suzune understands, is precisely what it means to be human. This perspective is explored more deeply when Suzune tries to convince Sudou to return during the sports festival. At that moment, she openly admits that she is not strong enough to do everything on her own. She acknowledges her own limitations, accepting that she is human and imperfect, and that she needs to rely on others.

By doing so, she recognizes Sudou as a vital component for collective victory. Suzune stops viewing strength as absolute self-sufficiency and begins to understand it as the ability to recognize, integrate, and value the strength of others. All humans are valuable.

   * Violence (Y1V1/Y1V6): The fact that Suzune does not openly engage in philosophical discussions does not mean she lacks interest in philosophy. Throughout the series, she is shown reading works that address complex and debatable themes, demonstrating a clear inclination toward intellectual reflection. A concrete example is “Crime and Punishment”, a novel centered on morality, guilt, and the justification of violence.

Her understanding of violence is neither superficial nor limited to the physical level. This is clearly reflected in how she perceives Ryuuen and his destructive potential. Suzune understands violence as a strategic tool—one used to impose control, instill fear, and destabilize social structures.

   * Truth (Y1V1/Y1V2/Y1V6/Y1V11.5): Suzune has confronted truth on many occasions throughout the series. For her, truth does not exist without evidence: a statement on its own holds no value unless it can be proven. This mindset is clearly reflected during Sudou’s trial, where she insists on uncovering the truth through Sakura’s testimony in order to prove Sudou’s innocence.

Over time, Suzune deepens her understanding of what truth truly entails. This learning process becomes especially pronounced in relation to Kushida. When Kiyotaka and Suzune theorize about how Kushida managed to destroy her former class without resorting to violence, they come to understand the power of truth as a weapon—an instrument capable of manipulation, division, and internal collapse. This realization is fully solidified after Kushida’s confession.

Ultimately, Suzune stops viewing truth solely as something to be wielded against others or as a strategic tool. She comes to understand that truth must also be directed inward. After spending most of her life chasing her brother’s image and suppressing her true self in an attempt to match him, Suzune is only able to move forward once she is honest with herself about what she truly wants.

Logical Mathematical Intelligence -
   * Academics (Y1V1-now): Suzune possesses a consistently high academic level, but even within that standard she stands out particularly in mathematics. Although she is not a genius in the strict sense, she managed to remain consistently within the top three of her year, reflecting not only intellectual ability but also discipline and stable performance. Furthermore, when she fully focuses on studying, she projects a strong sense of confidence that translates directly into results. This was clearly demonstrated during her bet against Kushida, where she scored a 98 on an exam specifically designed to confuse her and cause her to fail. In addition, she has a quick mind that allows her to perform complex calculations at high speed, a key asset both in academic evaluations and in strategic situations requiring immediate numerical reasoning.

Musical Intelligence -
   * ESE planning (Y1V11): Suzune can play the piano with enough confidence to defeat an opponent considered elite.

Interpersonal Intelligence -
   * Class D Leader (Y1V1-now): Suzune demonstrates a high capacity to understand others and interact with them effectively, both on an individual and collective level. This ability goes beyond superficial empathy and includes observation, behavioral analysis, and strategic adaptation of her communication.

A key example is her relationship with Kiyotaka. Through prolonged interaction, Suzune was able to identify his utilitarian view of people as tools, and later perceive more complex aspects of his personality: his enigmatic, curious, and even childish side. This understanding was not immediate, but the result of careful analysis of his words, actions, and omissions.

With Sudou, Suzune displayed active and functional empathy. She recognized his frustration, insecurity, and need for validation, offering him comfort and support during critical moments. This emotional understanding was essential in turning him into a loyal ally, not through coercion, but through acknowledging his individual value within the group.

In Hirata’s case, Suzune understood his emotional burden and his desire to protect class harmony. By identifying this, she provided him with exactly what he needed: a role that allowed him to feel useful and aligned with his principles, while also using him as an intermediary to convey her orders before she had fully established herself as leader.

Suzune also demonstrated social sensitivity by perceiving that Karuizawa was emotionally closer to Ayanokouji than she herself was, identifying that the cause was not rivalry, but rather the lack of full mutual trust between herself and Kiyotaka at the time. This precise reading of interpersonal dynamics highlights her ability to detect implicit social relationships.

However, Suzune is not limited to positive emotions. She is also capable of identifying, experiencing, and conveying negative emotions, as seen in her relationship with Kushida. Even without consciously remembering their shared past, Suzune displayed instinctive resentment, indicating a deep emotional perception that operates beyond explicit memory.

Lastly, her emotional understanding extends to the collective level. Suzune can anticipate how the class will react and what emotions will arise depending on her words or decisions. This was reflected in her strategic use of Hirata as a spokesperson, in the emotional manipulation that led to Yamauchi’s expulsion, and in how she guides the class under her strategies, adjusting her discourse to maximize acceptance, obedience, or cohesion depending on the situation.

   * False Cameras Strategy (Y1V2): She knew how to deal with and convince Ichinose to bring Class B into the plan, reading her pacifist intentions and using them to the advantage of the shared cause.

   * Hide Card Strategy (Y1V3): She knew how to articulate orders through Hirata and Ike so that Class D would function competently as a team, understanding what roles she should assign to each classmate to meet the basic needs of food and shelter on the island.

   * 'Skill-Based' system Strategy (Y1V5): She goes from being a completely isolated figure to directing the girls' physical evaluation and achieving functional integration with Sudou and Hirata.

   * Trap Kushida Strategy (Y1V6): She acknowledged her own low popularity following the Sports Festival. To remedy this, she enlisted Yōsuke Hirata as a mediator in the public eye, combining the logic of her plan with Hirata's charisma to secure the class's unanimous approval.

   * Expose Yamauchi (Y1V10): She sensed the latent social tensions and despair in the classroom. She knew exactly which chords to strike in each student (such as redirecting Sudou toward his personal growth) to sway public opinion.
Intrapersonal Intelligence -
   * She is herself (Y1V1-Y1V11.5): Since the later years of elementary school, Suzune began to imitate her older brother, Horikita Manabu, developing a habit that gradually became harmful. She paid complete attention to every word and action he took. She studied more, trained more, avoided doing anything he wouldn’t do, imitated his favorite foods and drinks, copied his favorite colors, and even adopted his style of clothing. Her path ceased to be her own and became almost entirely dependent on her brother’s will and image.

This dependency severely limited her development as an individual. Instead of exploring her own strengths, desires, and values, Suzune dedicated herself to chasing Manabu’s shadow, attempting to reach him and replicate him. As a result, she became the person we meet at the beginning of the story in Y1V1.

In the series start, Suzune was a selfish girl, incapable of teamwork and largely indifferent to others. She believed she could do everything alone and that relying on others was a weakness. However, over time and through multiple experiences, this mindset began to crack. She learned the value of teamwork, realized she was not a genius capable of carrying everything by herself, and acknowledged the importance of connecting with and appreciating the people around her as individuals.

This internal change had profound consequences. One of the most significant moments occurs when she must face the idea of choosing someone for expulsion. A decision she once would have approached coldly and rationally now became an emotionally burdensome task. But Suzune overcame her own aversion to expelling a classmate. She reflected internally, sought the necessary mental preparation by meeting with her brother Manabu and Ryuuen, and coldly accepted assuming the role of "judge" and the resulting resentment.

Standing in front of the mirror, Suzune reflected on her life and her relationship with Manabu. It was there that she recognized her fundamental mistake: for years, she tried to catch up to him, follow him, and become him. She lived by denying her own identity in order to become an incomplete copy.

But that phase had ended. Suzune realized she was no longer the girl who blindly chased her brother. She was not a cheap imitation nor an extension of his will. She was Horikita Suzune, with her own flaws, limits, and strengths.

With this realization, she chose to face her brother and finally reconcile with him. Letting go of past conflicts, she acknowledged her mistakes and, for the first time, released all the emotions she had suppressed for years. In that moment, Suzune stopped acting as the person she thought she needed to be and allowed her true self to emerge.

Strategy Categories:
   * Level the 3 idiots Strategy (Y1V1):

Objective: preventing the expulsion of the idiot trio before the midterm exams.

1-) STRATEGY EFFECTIVENESS:
   * Neither Ike, nor Yamauchi, nor Sudō were expelled, maintaining the integrity of the class.
   * The strategy succeeded in getting the three students to take their studies seriously and generated a lasting bond of respect and influence, particularly between Suzune and Sudō.
   * It allowed Suzune to discover Kiyotaka Ayanokōji's true ability, resources, and hidden potential.

2-) STRATEGY EFFICIENCY:
   * Suzune avoided adding endless, exhausting hours of studying from scratch. Instead, she turned the 10-minute breaks between classes into hyper-efficient learning blocks.
   * Aware that it was impossible to teach a complete lesson in 10 minutes to three low-achieving students, she filtered out the heavy theory and reduced it strictly to:
   * Address any specific questions gathered during the previous class.
   * Provide direct answers and the essential concepts needed to understand the day's material.
   * For the emergence of Sudō in English, she maximized the time spent / points obtained ratio by ordering him to memorize only the shortest answers that awarded the most points.
   * She understood that she couldn't single-handedly teach three people in such short intervals. To avoid exhausting her own time and energy, she integrated and delegated tasks to Kiyotaka and Kushida, optimizing the flow of explanation.
   * She spent a minimal investment of resources (paying for a drink for Kushida at Café Palette) to secure her cooperation, who in turn served as the social bait to attract and involve Ike and Yamauchi without Suzune having to beg them.
   * When she needed to buy the missing point to save Sudō from expulsion, Suzune didn't have to pay the full amount herself. Upon discovering Kiyotaka's scheme, she offered to buy a fraction of the point by supplementing Ayanokōji's points, thus saving her classmate's school life by splitting the financial cost.

3-) STRATEGY EXECUTION:
   1. Phase 1: Recruitment and Infrastructure
   1. Suzune summons Kushida to a cafe to restructure the study group, securing her support on the condition that she assist her in the future and using her as bait to lure Ike and Yamauchi.
   1. She directly confronts Ike, Yamauchi, and Sudō with the reality of their impending expulsion and declares that she will save them because she needs their points to reach Class A.
   2. Phase 2: Deployment of the Integrated Study System
   1. She designs a continuous cycle to turn the six hours of class into study, using the 10-minute breaks to explain key points about what they didn't understand in the previous period.
   1. She assimilates the day's answers during classes and delegates some of the explanations to Kiyotaka and Kushida to avoid teaching theory from scratch and focus only on essential concepts.
   2. She convinces Sudō by equating the study process with the discipline of basketball, paving the way for Kiyotaka to finalize the group's participation.
   3. During the library sessions, Suzune verbally neutralizes Yamawaki's (Class C) provocations to prevent Sudō from resorting to physical violence and losing focus.
   2. Upon discovering in the teachers' office that the exam syllabus had changed, she maintains an outward calm to contain the group's panic and take advantage of the crisis by increasing the three of their study commitment.
   3. Phase 3: Closure and Financial Safeguard
   1. Noticing that Sudō did not study English in time, she orders him to memorize only the shortest and highest-scoring answers to optimize his retention time.
   1. She intentionally reduces her grade lower class average and places Sudou in a position where a minimal adjustment will allow him to pass.
   2. She follows Kiyotaka after Sudō fails the English exam and discovers the possibility of buying points from Professor Sae.
   1. She contributes her own private points to complete the payment alongside Kiyotaka, buying the missing point on Sudō's note and definitively canceling his expulsion.

4-) STRATEGY RESILIENCE:
   * The most extreme test of resilience came when she discovered, through Chabashira-sensei in the teachers' lounge, that the exam topics initially reported had been completely changed. They had spent an entire week studying irrelevant material. Despite the emotional and internal impact, the resilience of her strategic framework allowed her to redirect the content without the methodology collapsing.
   * The strategy survived the three students' constant emotional and impulsive refusals. Sudō flatly rejected studying due to his resentment and hatred of academics. Suzune withstood this friction by reframing the narrative and adapting her discourse to compare studying to the discipline of basketball, which Sudō loved.

5-) STRATEGY SHIELDING
   * Suzune identified an insurmountable gap: it was impossible to teach complex theory from scratch in 10-minute breaks between classes. To address this shortcoming, she restructured her teaching: instead of explaining theory, she made sure to master the answers and key concepts from the day's classes so she could convey them directly and in a simplified way during breaks.
   * Noticing that she alone could not cover all three students simultaneously in such short intervals, she delegated part of the explanation to Kushida and Kiyotaka, closing the gap in individualized attention.
   * Suzune knew that her own cold and distant personality naturally repelled Ike, Yamauchi, and Sudō. To compensate for this communication breakdown, she brought Kushida to Café Palette and used her as a social buffer to lure the boys to the table and get them to accept her proposal.
   * Knowing that Sudō was incapable of memorizing the entire English exam at the last minute, Suzune devised a risk mitigation strategy: she advised him to focus solely on the shortest, highest-scoring answers. Furthermore, she intentionally lowered Sudō's previous average to place him in a zone where the passing curve was mathematically more attainable for him.

6-) STRATEGY FORESIGHT:
   * She correctly foresaw that if they spent their free time reviewing only old material, they would fall behind with the new subjects the teacher taught each day. Therefore, she structured the recess study group in real time to keep them up-to-date with the daily syllabus.
   * She accurately anticipated that Ike and Yamauchi would not join Hirata's group due to pride/social dynamics, and foresaw that the only way to convince them was by directly confronting them with the impending reality of their expulsion.
   * When Sudō failed and Chabashira left the classroom, Suzune realized that Kiyotaka's excuse about "going to the bathroom" was false. Discreetly following him, she anticipated that Kiyotaka would attempt a maneuver on his own, allowing her to intercept the negotiation and provide the necessary points to save Sudō.

7-) STRATEGY ADAPTABILITY:...
   * The classic method of studying in the library and long sessions collapsed due to Sudō's lack of discipline and initial resentment.
   * Suzune adapted the plan's structure to avoid wasting extended break time. She made room in her strategy to utilize the six hours of regular class time and divided the learning into 10-minute blocks during breaks. Instead of teaching all the theory from scratch, she adapted the content to focus solely on key answers and fundamental concepts.
   * Upon confirming with Sae that the exam topics had been changed at the last minute, everything studied during the week became useless.
   * Despite the internal crisis, Suzune didn't abandon her strategy. She capitalized on the emotional impact of the unexpected event to reframe the situation with the three idiots, managing to shift them from apathy to full commitment. Furthermore, she ensured the warning was spread throughout the entire class so that no one, even those outside her group, would be left behind.
   * Sudō neglected his English studies and did not have the cognitive ability or the time to memorize the entire exam.
   * During the break, she gave Sudō the objective of concentrating his few resources on memorizing only the shortest but highest-scoring answers; also, Suzune calculated and intentionally lowered her average so that Sudpu would have a chance to pass.
   * Sudō failed English by a single point and was officially expelled.
   * Suzune adapted her approach to the school's unwritten rules. By following Kiyotaka and understanding the point-buying mechanism with Professor Sae, she reacted immediately and combined her own points with Kiyotaka's to buy the point Sudō needed, thus overturning his expulsion.

8-) STRATEGIC ALIGNMENT:
   1. Suzune aligns her personal goal (reaching Class A) with the survival objective of the three idiots. She confronts Sudō, Ike, and Yamauchi, making it clear that she isn't helping them out of charity, but because losing members deducts points from the class. This eliminates the initial mistrust and creates a united front.
   2. To ensure the 10-minute break plan ran smoothly, Suzune aligned the roles of her allies: she used Kushida's social influence to summon and integrate the members, and delegated some of the explanatory workload to Kiyotaka. She prepared the responses during class, and the group processed them sequentially during the break, creating a seamless workflow.
   3. To fit Sudō's mindset (who hated studying) into the academic plan, Suzune aligned the concept of studying with the structure of basketball, which he loved. By translating academic preparation into terms of physical training and athletic discipline, she managed to get the most unstable member of the group to accept the strategy.

9-) TACTICAL COMPLEXITY:
   1. Layer 1: Kushida Alignment
   1. Suzune recognizes that she lacks the social reach and empathy necessary to summon Sudo, Ike, and Yamauchi on her own.
   1. She invites Kushida to Café Palette and pays for her drink to restructure the study group, framing it all within her personal goal of achieving Class A.
   2. She uses Kushida as the hook or "social bridge" to attract Ike and Yamauchi, anticipating their enthusiastic reaction upon seeing her.
   3. She accepts Kushida's condition to help her in the future in exchange for her immediate assistance in gathering and keeping the members motivated.
   2. Layer 2: Psychological Reframing and Deterrent Logic
   1. Once the three students are gathered, Suzune uses a combination of direct persuasion tactics and confrontation with reality:
   1. She confronts them directly, confirming that they are not in Hirata's group and that, without help, expulsion is inevitable.
   2. She uses her own ambition (to reach Class A) as a guarantee that she will not abandon them, transforming her individual goal into a lifeline for them.
   3. When Sudo shows reluctance due to his hatred of studying, Suzune adapts her language by comparing the learning process to basketball to break his mental resistance.
   3. Layer 3: Response Integration
   1. Given the short time before the exams, Suzune designs a learning structure divided into small blocks and delegated roles so as not to overload the 10-minute break:
   1. It turns the usual 6 hours of class that the three students used to waste into periods of writing down doubts.
   2. Knowing that she cannot teach complete theory in such a short time, Suzune ensures the exact answers to the day's questions during the class.
   3. She delegates some of the explanations to Kiyotaka and Kushida to divide the teaching load.
   4. Instead of teaching in-depth theory from scratch, they filter the teaching to focus strictly on the answers and essential concepts required to solve the lesson of the day.
   4. Layer 4: Containment Tactics
   1. When Yamawaki (Class C) tries to destabilize and mock the group, Suzune intervenes and verbally destroys him.
   1. Her intervention restrains Sudo in time, preventing the discussion from escalating into a physical confrontation that would have resulted in sanctions or Sudo's immediate expulsion before the exam.
   2. When Sudou forgets to study English and puts himself in danger of expulsion
   1. Given Sudo's lack of time for the English test, Suzune advises him to memorize only the shortest but highest-scoring answers to maximize his chances.
   2. Knowing that Sudo won't be able to memorize the entire exam, Suzune intentionally decides to lower Sudo's grade to reduce his average and try to give him a chance to pass.
   3. After discreetly following Kiyotaka and discovering that he handed over the past exams to Kushida, Suzune intervenes in the negotiation between Kiyotaka and Sae, putting up her own points to buy the missing point and definitively ensure Sudo's permanence.

Personal ranking for the best strategy categories in this feat:
Efficiency > Execution > Shielding > Alignment > Adaptability >  Tactical > Effectiveness > Foresight > Resilience

   * False Cameras Strategy (Y1V2):

1-) STRATEGY EFFECTIVENESS:
   * The three aggressors/accusers from Class C (Ishizaki, Komiya, and Kondō) completely surrendered to the psychological trap and withdrew their complaint against Sūdō to the school, ensuring a complete victory for Class D.
   * The strategy was designed so that no one would be expelled or physically punished. The real culprits ended up paying the price for wasting the institution's time without being able to retaliate.
   * Actively involving Class B not only demonstrated trust in them, but also formally solidified the strategic alliance between Class D and Class B, establishing Kiyotaka as the link between the two.
   * While the trap was being set in the special building, Suzune remained in the student council room monitoring Sūdō. This prevented him from making any last-minute mistakes and fulfilled her goal of making him understand that he needed to change his impulsive attitude if he wanted to avoid future problems.
   * Suzune reinforced the strategy by assigning Kanzaki the task of buying the fake camera and Ichinose (Class B) the main execution. In the event the school discovered the deception, the responsibility and consequences would fall entirely on Class B, keeping Class D and Sūdō completely safe from retaliation. (Interpretation)

2-) STRATEGY EFFICIENCY:
   * Class D didn't spend any private points to buy the camera or set up the scenery. Suzune had Kanzaki (Class B) finance the acquisition of the fake camera with her own points, reducing the material cost for her class to zero.
   * Instead of creating complex tests, the strategy leveraged existing climatic and logistical resources:
   * She scheduled the meeting for 3:40 PM in the special building to take advantage of the sweltering heat of the sun (25-30 °C) and instructed everyone to turn off the air conditioning. This environmental factor clouded the accusers' judgment and reduced their capacity for critical thinking without wasting energy on lengthy confrontations.
   * She used the technical assistance of the class technology expert (Sotomura) to quickly and cleanly place the camera in a discreet spot.
   * Efficient Division of Labor and Delegation:
   * She delegated to Kushida the task of summoning the students from Class C.
   * She used Ichinose's charisma, popularity, and social skills, along with Kiyotaka's presence, to corner the students, leaving them no room to argue or ask for outside help.
   * While Ichinose and Kiyotaka executed the deception on the ground, Suzune optimized her time by remaining in the student council room, simultaneously resolving Sūdō's containment and the lesson on his behavior.

3-) STRATEGY EXECUTION:
   1. Phase 1: Resource Alignment
   1. Suzune meets with Ichinose and Kanzaki after the trial to define the tasks. Instead of accepting the initial offer to find new witnesses, she refines and distributes the roles to ensure flawless execution.
   1. Assign Kanzaki the task of purchasing a fake camera using his own private points.
   2. Design the operation so that, if the ANHS discovers the deception, the responsibility falls entirely on Class B (since they purchased the device and Ichinose would assume the main active role).
   3. By giving Class B a leading role, project confidence in the alliance, protecting Class D from the consequences in the worst-case scenario and strengthening the relationship in the best.
   2. Phase 2: Device Installation and Site Preparation
   1. The physical preparation of the trap requires the intervention of technical and environmental elements to condition the victims:
   1. With Sotomura's help, they strategically install the fake camera in a discreet location inside the special building where the original fight took place.
   2. The meeting is scheduled for 3:40 PM, immediately after classes end. At that time, the sun beats down directly on the building, raising the internal temperature to 25-30°C. With the air conditioning turned off, the stifling heat impairs the aggressors' judgment and ability to react.
   3. She assigns Kushida the task of summoning Ishizaki, Komiya, and Kondō to the special building at precisely 3:40 PM. Kushida's presence or summons ensures they arrive at the agreed-upon location without anticipating a hostile confrontation or a trap.
   3. Phase 3: Deployment of the Execution Team and Psychological Pressure
   1. For the direct execution of the deception on the ground, Suzune deploys Kiyotaka and Ichinose, selected for their complementary profiles:
   1. Ichinose's charisma, popularity, and social skills drastically reduce the room for maneuver or discussion for the boys in Class C.
   2. Kiyotaka and Ichinose confront them directly, making them believe that the installed camera captured the entire fight from the beginning.
   3. By combining the impact of "visual evidence", social pressure and exhaustion from the sweltering heat, the duo systematically prevents the students in Class C from seeking outside help or developing an escape alibi.
   4. Cornered by the alleged camera evidence and under close monitoring by Kiyotaka and Ichinose to prevent escape attempts, Ishizaki, Komiya, and Kondō give in and formally withdraw their complaint to the school.
   5. The outcome forces the guilty parties to face the consequences of wasting the institution's time, resolving the conflict without expulsions for Class D or penalties for Class B.
   4. Phase 5: Internal Containment and Sudō Management
   1. While the operation is being carried out in the special building, Suzune personally takes control of the internal risk variables within the council:
   1. Suzune remains in the student council room, keeping Ken Sūdō under strict control.
   2. Her presence prevents Sudō from making an outburst or an impulsive last-minute mistake that could ruin the validity of the agreement or the withdrawal of the complaint.
   3. She takes advantage of this tactical confinement to fulfill her initial pedagogical objective, which was to make Sūdō understand that he must change his impulsive attitude to avoid future problems.

4-) STRATEGY RESILIENCE:
   * The strategy did not rely solely on the device's bluff. It summoned the culprits (Ishizaki, Komiya, and Kondō) at 3:40 PM in the special building, taking advantage of the fact that at that time direct solar radiation raised the interior temperature to 25-30 °C without air conditioning.
   * The sweltering heat clouded the targets' judgment, reducing their critical reasoning ability and stifling their desire to debate the veracity of the camera, making the strategy withstand any attempt at logical counter-argumentation by the criminals.
   * The choice of Ichinose and Kiyotaka to confront them created a barrier of social resistance. Ichinose's charisma, popularity, and impeccable reputation eliminated the possibility of the boys trying to intimidate them or accuse them of lying, forcing them into a tragic retreat.

5-) STRATEGY SHIELDING
   * If the school administration discovers that deceptive tactics and a fake camera were used to extort the withdrawal of the complaint, the blame will fall entirely on Class B.
   * Since Class B bought the fake camera with their own points and Ichinose carried out the direct confrontation, in the worst case scenario Class D would be completely clean and exempt from punishment, while only Class B would suffer the consequences.
   * By actively involving Class B at the core of the plan, Suzune demonstrated absolute trust in them. This shielded the strategy from potential betrayals by Class B and strengthened the long-term bilateral relationship in the event of resounding success.
   * The design ensured that no student (neither from Class D nor Class C) would be physically harmed or directly expelled, forcing the real culprits to pay for wasting the institution's time without leaving room for direct retaliation.
   * While the plan was being executed in the special building, Suzune physically remained in the student council room monitoring Sūdou.
   * This move shielded the trap from Sūdō's volatile temper, preventing him from making a last-second outburst that would ruin the play or cause him to panic.
   * At the same time, she achieved her goal of making him understand that he needed to change his aggressive attitude to avoid getting into future trouble, creating a preventative shield for the upcoming exams.

6-) STRATEGY FORESIGHT:
   * She accurately predicted that Ishizaki, Komiya, and Kondō would have no room for argument or defense, believing that a security camera had recorded the entire incident from the beginning.
   * She anticipated that the combination of Ichinose's charismatic authority, Kiyotaka's stoic presence, and the psychological stress caused by the extreme heat would inevitably lead to the voluntary withdrawal of the complaint for fear of being discovered and punished.
   * She anticipated that the combination of Ichinose's charismatic authority, Kiyotaka's stoic presence, and the psychological stress caused by the extreme heat would inevitably lead to the voluntary withdrawal of the complaint for fear of being discovered and punished.
   * She realized that by actively involving Class B in the plan, she was not only solving the immediate problem, but also demonstrating mutual trust and formally solidifying the alliance between Class D and Class B.
   * She established Kiyotaka as the direct bridge of communication between both classes, ensuring a permanent channel of strategic cooperation for the future.
   * She predicted that winning the trial through trickery would be pointless if Sudō's behavior remained unchanged. She designed the strategy not only as a trap for Class C, but also as a pre-trial educational tool to teach Sudō that he needed to change his attitude if he wanted to avoid future problems at school.

7-) STRATEGY ADAPTABILITY:
   * Suzune left a strategic "safety space" in case the school discovered the deceptive maneuver.
   * If the deception was exposed, all legal responsibility would fall on Class B. It was Kanzaki who purchased the fake camera using his personal points, and Ichinose who played the most active and intimidating role at the scene of the incident. This completely shielded Class D from direct disciplinary repercussions should the scheme fail.
   * Aware that Sudou's volatility and impulsiveness could ruin the success of the negotiation at the last minute if he interfered or made a mistake in the special building, Suzune adapted by assigning herself a separate defensive role.
   * She remained in the student council room monitoring Sūdō in real time to prevent him from interfering and to ensure that he understood the lesson about his attitude.
   * She adjusted the execution of the confrontation to the 3:40 PM timeframe, using the external weather conditions to cloud the judgment of the Class C boys and hasten the surrender without the need for physical contact or additional confrontations.
   * Given the possibility that Ishizaki, Komiya, and Kondō might try to ask for outside help or flee when they felt cornered, the strategy involved close and continuous surveillance by Ichinose and Kiyotaka, strategically preventing any last-minute escape attempts.

8-) STRATEGIC ALIGNMENT:
   1. Resource Alignment
   1. She purchased the fake camera using Kanzaki's points and discreetly installed it thanks to the technical support of "Professor" Sotomura in the special building.
   2. She used Kushida's friendly image to successfully summon the aggressors (Ishizaki, Komiya, and Kondō) to the exact location at 3:40 PM after classes ended.
   3. She used Ichinose's undeniable charisma and status alongside Kiyotaka for direct confrontation, socially cornering the Class C boys without giving them room to doubt or react.
   2. Alignment of Environmental Conditions
   1. The timing (3:40 PM) was not random. The plan aligned the position of the sun shining directly on the building with the air conditioning being turned off, raising the interior temperature to 25-30 °C.
   2. The sweltering heat clouded the criminals' judgment, preventing them from thinking clearly or deducing the camera's falsity amidst the stressful state.
   3. Alignment of Multiple Objectives
   1. Force the immediate withdrawal of the complaint against Sudo.
   2. Suzune stayed in the student council room monitoring Sudo to prevent outbursts of anger and teach him a moral lesson about his impulsive attitude.
   3. Strengthen the political relationship with Class B by demonstrating mutual trust.

9-) TACTICAL COMPLEXITY:
   1. Layer 1: The Physical Assembly and Visual Deception
   1. Obtain a fake camera, purchased by Kanzaki with his points, and discreetly install it with Sotomura's technical assistance in the special building where the altercation occurred.
   1. The camera doesn't need to be real or connected to a network; its purpose is purely utilitarian within the trap to create an illusion of irrefutable evidence that leaves no room for maneuver or excuse for the aggressors.
   2. Layer 2: Manipulation and Environmental Conditioning
   1. She summoned Class 1-C at exactly 3:40 PM, immediately after classes, with the air conditioning off. At that time, the sun shines directly on the special building, raising the internal temperature to 25–30 °C.
   1. This overlapping physical layer directly affects the aggressors' psychology. The stifling heat clouds their judgment, reduces their critical thinking skills, and increases their anxiety. This prevents them from thoroughly analyzing whether the camera is real or if they are falling for a bluff.
   3. Layer 3: Ambush by Social Authority and Non-Imputability
   1. Deploy Honami and Kiyotaka to confront the students of Class C.
   1. Ichinose brings charisma, social standing, and an impeccable reputation for honesty to the first year. For the students of Class C, it's extremely difficult to accuse or doubt the word of someone with Ichinose's public profile.
   2. Kiyotaka and Ichinose watch them closely to actively block any attempt at escape or external communication before they can ask Ryūen or their teachers for help.
   4. Layer 4: Containment and Re-education of Sudou
   1. While the confrontation is taking place in the special building, Suzune remains in the Student Council room keeping Sudou under control.
   1. This layer prevents Sudou from making a last-minute mistake or reacting violently, thus ruining the agreement. At the same time, Suzune uses the situation to make him understand that he must change his impulsive attitude if he wants to avoid future expulsions.
   5. Layer 5: Damage Reduction
   1. Delegate the purchase of the camera to Kanzaki and put Ichinose in charge of executing the bluff.
   1. If the school discovered the deception or fraudulent tactic, all responsibility would fall on Class 1-B (who purchased the camera and led the event). This ensured that Class 1-D was 100% protected in the worst-case scenario. Furthermore, involving Class B strengthened the alliance between the two classes.

   * Hide Card Strategy (Y1V3):

1-) STRATEGY EFFECTIVENESS:
   * In theory and in its initial execution, Suzune's strategy was remarkably effective. She managed to deceive her 39 classmates and the leaders and co-leaders of the other classes about her true state of health. She accepted the leadership role to prevent it from falling into inefficient hands, stayed near the camp or in her tent with the card in her pocket, and when she went to the points machine, she was accompanied by someone else to confuse the opposition and Ibuki.
   * In practice, the direct effectiveness of her plan crumbled due to factors that clouded her judgment (a five-day fever) and Kiyotaka's machinations. Ibuki managed to steal the card from her pocket while Suzune was washing up after Yamauchi's mud prank. Although she pursued Ibuki that night despite her fever and managed to deduce through provocation that Ibuki was the thief and not the one responsible for the burned manual, she lost the physical fight due to exhaustion, falling unconscious and permanently losing control of the card.

2-) STRATEGY EFFICIENCY:
   * Suzune's strategy was based on spending as few points as possible and protecting the card while ensuring basic survival.
   * Noticing Ike's camping skills and how he was marginalized by Shinohara, Suzune delegated to Kiyotaka the task of convincing Ike to lead the fieldwork, rescuing a valuable resource for the class.
   * She organized the foraging group into pairs to optimize exploration time in the forest.
   * She used the presence of Hirata and Karuizawa as a mandatory cooperation bridge to keep the class cohesive, despite not fully trusting Hirata because she considered him "two-faced".

3-) STRATEGY EXECUTION:
   1. Phase 1: Assuming the Leader Role
   1. After hearing the rules on the first day, Suzune devises a simple strategy: to keep the leader's identity hidden while accumulating points on the registration machine, spending as little as possible. When Kushida proposes her as leader, Suzune readily accepts the role to prevent the responsibility from falling on someone incapable, like one of the "three idiots." From this point on, she translates her defensive thinking into action, keeping the Target Card in her pocket or close to her body, and remaining either in her shop or outside to ensure its constant physical protection.
   2. Phase 2: Internal Class Management and Task Assignment
   1. Suzune acknowledges that she can't easily collaborate with a fragmented class and notes that Hirata, while close to a social leader, isn't taking the necessary steps to save them. However, she analyzes their surroundings and recognizes the practical value of Ike's camping skills. To get the class functioning without directly exposing herself, she tasks Kiyotaka with convincing Ike to help, thus ensuring the collection of resources and basic infrastructure for Class D's survival.
   3. Phase 3: Reconnaissance of Enemy Camps and Alliances
   1. To gather strategic information, Suzune conducts direct visits to the bases of the other classes:
   1. Class C: Accepts the invitation alongside Kiyotaka, calmly confronts Ryūen's provocations, deduces that he does not plan to save points, and evaluates the excuse regarding Ibuki's departure.
   2. Class B: Negotiate an alliance with Ichinose based on previous contacts, observing the cohesion of her class, learning tricks from her to save points, and detecting a member of Class C among them.
   3. Class A: Deploys a sharp verbal persuasion tactic in front of Yahiko at the cave entrance. Although Katsuragi interrupts his access, the pressure exerted by Suzune allows him to extract key information about the presence of the reclaiming device inside the cave.
   4. Phase 4: Internal Crisis Management
   1. When Karuizawa's underwear is stolen and the group of girls panics, Suzune logically intervenes to prevent the class from splitting. She proposes alternative hypotheses to neutralize Karuizawa's attacks and demands that Kiyotaka accompany Hirata if he moves the girls' belongings, undermining their blind trust in him by pointing to him as a suspect. By also exposing the girls' secret purchases made with Hirata's help, she weakens the internal narrative and manages to impose discipline on the class, at least temporarily.
   2. During the exam, Suzune employs a deliberate diversionary tactic: each time she goes to the scoring machine, she is accompanied by others. The purpose of this calculated action is to confuse outside observers (and Ibuki in particular), preventing them from obtaining conclusive evidence of who the true leader of Class D is.
   3. Despite battling a severe fever (37.5°C - 38°C) for five days, Suzune orchestrates a massive deception on 39 of her comrades and the leaders of the enemy classes, acting normally so as not to become a burden. Even with her cognitive abilities impaired, she organizes the foraging party into pairs to optimize time and resources.
   4. When Ibuki takes advantage of Suzune's bath time to steal her card, and after witnessing the manual being set on fire, Suzune connects the two events and notices Ibuki's escape. In the middle of the night, she stealthily tracks Ibuki for 100 meters through the vegetation despite a fever of 38°C. When intercepted, she engages in hand-to-hand combat with Ibuki (an expert in martial arts) and uses verbal provocation as a tactical tool to confirm three crucial facts: that Ibuki stole the underwear, that she stole the leader's card, and that she did not burn the manual.

4-) STRATEGY RESILIENCE:
   * Despite suffering from a high fever (37.5°C to 38°C) for the entire five days of the exam, her defense plan didn't collapse. She managed to feign complete normalcy and act naturally in front of her 39 classmates, as well as the leaders and co-leaders of Classes A, B, and C. No one on the island (except Kiyotaka and Ibuki after their fight) noticed her condition.
   * Although her mind functioned at less than half its usual capacity and exhaustion consumed her, she maintained the operational discipline of keeping the Target Card in her pocket, limiting her outings, and performing each scanner check accompanied to maintain the deception.
   * The class lacked leadership and was uncooperative. When Hirata proved incapable of making tough decisions and the group split over the theft of Karuizawa's underwear, Suzune's strategy withstood the pressure of collective panic.

5-) STRATEGY SHIELDING
   * When Kushida proposed Suzune as leader and Sudou volunteered, Suzune immediately accepted the role as a cover. Her goal was to prevent any of the "three idiots" from getting hold of the card, knowing that their incompetence or lack of caution would expose it and cause the class's automatic defeat.
   * To cover up any leaks about her role, Suzune intentionally brought someone else with her whenever she went to the machine to claim points. This created a smokescreen meant to confuse both the enemy and Ibuki.
   * She kept the card safely tucked away in her pocket inside her tent or on her person, establishing security measures that, under normal circumstances and without the factor of weakness from fever, made it impossible for a third party to obtain evidence of her leadership.
   * Noticing Ike's survival skills but recognizing herself as incompetent to convince him after the girls' hostile treatment, Suzune covered up that shortcoming by sending Kiyotaka to persuade him, securing camping knowledge for the class.
   * Upon identifying Hirata as a "two-faced" figure who was not entirely trustworthy, she implemented a control measure, demanding that Kiyotaka accompany him when moving the girls' belongings to maintain mutual supervision and prevent any maneuvering behind the group's back.
   * Upon discovering the Target Card theft after washing off the mud Yamauchi had thrown at her, she didn't ignore the vulnerability. After deducing that the plume of smoke (a burned manual) and the theft were connected to Ibuki's disappearance, she initiated a stealth chase through the jungle in the darkness for 100 meters.
   * When confronted by Ibuki (a martial arts expert), Suzune faced the aggression head-on despite her 38°C fever. During the fight, she used calculated verbal provocations as a tactic to get Ibuki to talk.

Furthermore, anticipating that her visits to the points machine might reveal her identity if she went alone, she made sure to always be accompanied by someone else to confuse the surveillance of other classes and Ibuki.

Anticipating social manipulation, she formulated logical hypotheses. This prevented Karuizawa's impulsive outburst and ensured Hirata's support for their operational stance.
   * Running a fever of 37.5°C to 38°C since the first day, Suzune foresaw that showing weakness would make her health a vulnerability for the class and a prime target for enemy spies. She planned to act naturally and distance herself from her classmates' condition, successfully deceiving all 39 students of Class D and the leaders of the rival classes throughout the exam.
